Kingdee International Software Group Company Limited
              金蝶國際軟件集團有限公司
                Stock Code 股份代號 : 268




                                        Annual Report

                                        2019 年報
Corporate Introduction
公司簡介

Shares of the Company are listed on the Main Board of the Hong Kong Stock Exchange (stock code: 0268) and the Company is
headquartered in Shenzhen, the PRC. Adhering to the core values of “Acting in all Conscience, with Integrity and Righteousness”,
the Company is committed to helping businesses achieve their growth targets and let the sun shine on every company through
dedicated services. It strives to provide them with the most trusted enterprise service platform. Through persistent efforts to
explore China’s Cloud enterprise service market, Kingdee has retained the largest share in the enterprise application software
sector for fast-growing enterprises for 15 consecutive years, and has grasped the biggest share in the enterprise-grade SaaS
Cloud services industry for the second year, also the Company has grasped the biggest share in the enterprise-grade SaaS ERM
(as known as Cloud ERP) and Financial Cloud services industry for the third year.

In addition, Kingdee’s diverse Cloud services and products are the preferred choices of leading enterprises. They include “Kingdee
Cloud Cosmic” (a Cloud service platform for large enterprises), “Kingdee Cloud Galaxy” (a digital innovative Cloud service
platform for medium and large enterprises and fast-growing enterprises), “Kingdee Jingdou Cloud” (one-stop Cloud services
platform for micro and small-sized enterprises), “Cloud-Hub” (intelligent Cloud office), “Guanyi Cloud” (Cloud services for
E-commerce operators), “Cargeer” (Cloud services for auto dealers) and “Wojia Cloud”(Cloud services for Property Industry).
With its strengths in management software and Cloud services, Kingdee provides services and products to more than 6.8 million
enterprises, government agencies and other organizations around the world.

本公司是香港聯交所主板上市公司（股票代碼：0268），總部位於中國深圳。以「致良知、走正道、行王道」為核心
價值觀，以「全心全意為企業服務，讓陽光照進每一個企業」為使命，致力成為「最值得託付的企業服務平台」。金
蝶在中國企業雲服務市場不斷探索，IDC資料顯示，金蝶不僅連續15年穩居中國成長型企業應用軟件市場佔有率第
一、更連續2年在中國企業級應用SaaS雲服務市場佔有率排名第一，連續3年在中國企業級SaaS ERM（即雲ERP）、財務
雲市場佔有率第一。金蝶是目前唯一入選Gartner全球市場指南(Market Guide)的中國企業SaaS雲服務廠商。

金蝶旗下的多款雲服務產品獲得標桿企業的青睞，包括金蝶雲 • 蒼穹（大企業數字共生平台）、金蝶雲 • 星空（成
長型企業數位化平台）、金蝶精鬥雲（小微企業成長服務平台）、雲之家（智慧協同雲）、管易雲（企業電商雲服務平
台）、車商悅（汽車經銷行業雲）及我家雲（物業行業雲）等。金蝶通過管理軟件與雲服務，已為世界範圍內超過680
萬家企業、政府等組織提供服務。

Chairman’s Statement
主席報告


In 2019, we built a thriving corporate culture. The core value of Kingdee            2019年，是金蝶文化突圍的一年。《金蝶哲學》的核心是：「以
Philosophy is “Kingdee strives for excellence and customer satisfaction,             奮鬥者為本、以客戶為中心，企業經營者對成長的渴望，就
ultimately fulfilling customers’ demand for growth and success.” We believe          是我們的奮鬥目標」。一個企業全面雲化的時代真正來臨，
that digitally transforming enterprises is the leading factor driving an era of      雲業務的本質特徵是建立以客戶成功為中心、長期可持續發
greater cloud adoption. SaaS is built upon cloud business ecosystem focusing         展、長期提供高價值服務的行業生態系統。金蝶將持續建設
on customer success centered, sustainable growth and high value services.            One Kingdee、One Family、One Culture三大支撐體系，把陽光、
Kingdee is committed to construct “One Kingdee, One Family, One Culture”,            溫暖和愛帶給每一個用戶。
bringing love and care to every customer.

Outlook                                                                              展望
During the 2020 Chinese New Year, the pneumonia epidemic caused by the               新年伊始，雖然新型冠狀病毒疫情對各行各業有所衝擊，
novel coronavirus (COVID-19) affected all sectors of the society in China, but       但是，我們對企業雲服務行業長期發展充滿信心。國家、
we harbor confidence in the long-term development of enterprise cloud services       企業、個人將加速擁抱雲轉型和移動辦公。越來越多的傳統
industry. The epidemic will be a catalyst to the adoption of cloud transformation    企業必將充分利用數字化新技術加速數字化轉型，走向線上

     Credit risk                                                                          信貸風險
     Credit risk arises from cash and cash equivalents, contractual cash flows            信 用 風 險 源 於 現 金 及 現 金 等 價 物、 以 攤 餘 成 本 計 量
     of debt instruments carried at amortised cost, at fair value through profit          的 債 務 工 具 合 同 現 金 流 量、 以 公 允 價 值 計 量 且 其 變
     or loss (FVPL) and deposits with banks and financial institutions, as well           動 計 入 損 益 的 債 務 工 具 合 同 現 金 流 量、 銀 行 和 其
     as credit exposures to customers, including outstanding receivables.                 他金融機構存款以及未收應收款在內的客戶信用
                                                                                          風險。


     The management manages the credit risk of cash and cash equivalents,                 為管理來自現金及現金等價物、抵押存款、短期銀行存
     pledged and short-term bank deposits and wealth management products                  款及理財產品（歸類為以公允價值計量且其變動計入損
     (classified as financial assets at FVPL) by transacting with state-owned             益的金融資產）的信用風險，本集團管理層僅與中國國
     financial institutions and reputable commercial banks which are all                  有或信譽良好的中國大陸及香港的財務機構進行交易。
     high-credit-quality financial institutions in the PRC and Hong Kong.

     In relation to trade receivables and contract assets, the Group has two              對於應收賬款及合同資產，本集團有兩種銷售管道，一
     kinds of distribution channels, one is sales to distributors and the other is        種是銷售給經銷商，另一種是銷售給最終客戶。
     sales to end customers.

     For distributors, the Group has assessed the credit quality of the                   對於銷售給經銷商，本集團評估經銷商的信用品質會考
     distributors, taking into account their financial position, past experience          慮其財務狀況、信用歷史記錄及其他因素。根據評估的
     and other factors. Individual risk limits are set based on internal                  信用品質設定相應的信用額度。管理層亦採取一定的監
     or external ratings in accordance with limits set by the board. The                  控程式確保經銷商在信用額度內採購。
     compliance with credit limits by distributors is regularly monitored by
     management.

     For sales to end customers, the Group has no significant concentration               對於銷售給最終客戶，因應收賬款非常零星且分佈於大
     of credit risk in trade receivables since the balance of trade receivables is        量的消費者中，本集團於應收賬款並無集中的信貸風
     composed of numerous individual small items and the exposure spreads                 險。
     over a large number of customers.

     The Group’s investments in debt instruments, including loans to third                本集團的債務工具投資，包括給予第三方的貸款和給予
     parties and related parties are considered to be low risk investments. The           關聯方的貸款，被判定為低風險投資。為了防止信貸惡
     credit ratings of the investments are monitored for credit deterioration.            化，這些投資的信用評級收到了檢測。


     For other receivables, management makes periodic collective assessment               對於其他應收款項，管理層根據歷史結算記錄和以往經
     as well as individual assessment on the recoverability of the receivables            驗，對應收款項的可收回性進行定期的集體評估和個別
     based on historical settlement record and past experience. The directors             評估。本公司董事認為，本集團其他應收款項的未清償
     of the Company believe that there is no material credit risk inherent in the         餘額不存在重大的內在信用風險。
     Group’s outstanding balances of other receivables.

Share Award Scheme                                                                             股份獎勵計劃

the Company on 4 December 2015 to provide incentives and reward the                            勵計劃」）以提供激勵以及獎勵本集團若干僱員及董事所作

Scheme shall be valid and effective for a period of 10 years commencing from                   份獎勵計劃之詳情請參考日期為二零一五年十二月十八日之
the adoption date. For details of the Share Award Scheme, please refer to the                  公佈。
announcement of the Company dated 18 December 2015.

award shares pursuant to the Share Award Scheme, of which a total of 6,450,000                 劃授出共計19,178,000股獎勵股份，本公司董事仍有尚未歸屬

Corporate Governance Practices                                                     企業管治常規
The Group consistently promotes good corporate governance, pursuing the            本集團一直以來致力於維持良好的企業管治，奉行確保股東
five principles of ensuring the rights and interests of shareholders, abiding      權益，恪守董事會和管理層職責，履行全面披露與提高透明
by the directives of the Board and management, promoting full disclosure and       度之責任，平等對待相關利益者，實施有效的內控與監督的
transparency, ensuring the equal treatment of stakeholders, and strengthening      五大原則，努力提升企業價值，確保公司保持長期穩定的發
internal controls and supervision. The Company endeavors to enhance its            展，保障股東的利益。下文詳細闡述本公司企業管治常規。
enterprise value, ensure the Company’s long-term and stable development
and safeguard the interests of its shareholders. The details of the corporate

      a view to furthering the Shareholders’ long-term interests.

Adoption of Code of Conduct Regarding Director’s Securities                        採納有關董事進行證券交易的標準守則
Transactions

Directors’ securities transactions on terms no less exacting than the required     易的行為守則（「行為守則」），行為守則的標準不遜於上市規

Internal Control and Risk Management                                                   內部控制及風險管理
1.    Risk Management and Internal Control Governance Framework                        1.    風險管理及內部監控治理架構
      The Company has an internal audit function that is used to safeguard the               公司設有內部審核功能，以確保本集團設立與維持合適
      Group’s establishment and maintenance of an appropriate and effective                  及有效的風險管理與內部監控系統。該內部監控系統為
      system for risk management and internal control, which is a system                     經營管理而設計，而不能完全消除為達到商業目標而可
      designed to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve                能存在失敗的風險，也僅能夠提供合理但不是唯一的能
      business objectives, and can only provide reasonable and not absolute                  夠抵抗重大失誤和損失的保證。
      assurance against material misstatement or loss.

      The Board is responsible for the said system for risk management and                   董事會對所述風險管理和內部監控系統負責，並檢討其
      internal control and reviewing its effectiveness. The Board reviews the                有效性。董事會每年檢討本公司風險管理和內部監控制
      Company’s risk management and internal control system annually and                     度，並採取必要與適當措施以維持內該系統，監控風險
      will take necessary and appropriate measures to maintain such system                   管理成效，以保障股東投資及本公司資產。
      and control the effect of risk management for the purpose of safeguarding
      shareholders’ investments and the Company’s assets.

      The management has specially established an internal audit department,                 管理層專設內審部負責對公司的風險管理、內部監控系
      which is responsible for supervising and auditing the Company’s risk                   統和業務運營等進行監督與審計，利用內部審計程式，
      management, internal control system and business operation and using                   進行獨立評估。同時業務組織與職能運營部門根據職
      internal audit procedures to conduct independent assessment. At the same               責，識別、評估及應對本部門負責的風險，在各業務及
      time, business organization and functional operation departments identify,             職能運營範疇，執行風險管理程式及內部監控措施，並
      assess and handle the risks in their charge according to their duties and              由管理層向董事會報告。
      carry out risk management procedures and internal control measures
      within their respective business and functional operation scope, and the
      management reports to the Board.

            of their impact and screen out major risks;

    and control to prevent or appease the possibility of occurrence of any

    year. Due to the fierce market competition of the industry, the Company’s           行業市場競爭較為激烈，公司在重點客戶與雲業務方面

the objects of the meetings and signed by the requisitionists, provided that        於本公司股東大會表決權的不少於十分之一繳足股本。
such requisitionists hold as at the date of deposit of the requisition not less
than one-tenth of the paid up capital of the Company which carries the right of

proceed duly to convene the meeting to be held within a further 21 days, the        開將予在其後的二十一日內舉行的大會，則請求人自身或代

the total voting rights, may convene the general meeting in the same manner,        董事會召開大會的相同方式召開股東大會，惟按上述方式召

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the                  我們的目標，是對合併財務報表整體是否不存在由於欺詐或

no other purpose. We do not assume responsibility towards or accept liability        其他人士負上或承擔任何責任。合理保證是高水準的保證，
to any other person for the contents of this report. Reasonable assurance is         但不能保證按照《國際審計準則》進行的審計，在某一重大錯
a high level of assurance, but is not a guarantee that an audit conducted in         誤陳述存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引起，
accordance with ISAs will always detect a material misstatement when it exists.      如果合理預期它們單獨或匯總起來可能影響合併財務報表使

individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected to influence     述可被視作重大。

2    Summary of significant accounting policies (continued)                          2     重要會計政策摘要（續）
2.2 Changes in accounting policies                                                   2.2   會計政策變更
     This note explains the impact of the adoption of IFRS 16 Leases on the                本附註介紹了採納《國際財務報告準則第16號 － 租賃》
     Group’s financial statements.                                                         對本集團財務報表的影響。

     As indicated in Note 2.1 above, the Group has adopted IFRS 16 Leases                  如上文附註2.1所述，本集團自二零一九年一月一日起
     retrospectively from 1 January 2019, but has not restated comparatives                追溯適用《國際財務報告準則第16號 － 租賃》，但是根
     for the 2018 reporting period, as permitted under the specific transition             據準則包含的具體過渡規定，並未對2018報告期間的比
     provisions in the standard. The reclassifications and the adjustments                 較數字進行重述。因此，因新租賃準則產生的重分類及
     arising from the new leasing rules are therefore recognised in the opening            調整在二零一九年一月一日期初財務狀況表內確認。關
     consolidated statement of financial position on 1 January 2019. The new               於新會計政策的披露，詳見附註2.31。
     accounting policies are disclosed in Note 2.31.

     On adoption of IFRS 16, the Group recognised lease liabilities in relation            因採用《國際財務報告準則第16號 － 租賃》，本集團為
     to leases which had previously been classified as ‘operating leases’ under            前期根據國際會計準則第17號 － 租賃歸入「經營租賃」
     the principles of IAS 17 Leases. These liabilities were measured at the               分類的租賃確認租賃負債。承租人應以剩餘租賃付款額
     present value of the remaining lease payments, discounted using the                   按二零一九年一月一日的承租人增量借款利率折現的現
     lessee’s incremental borrowing rate as of 1 January 2019. The weighted                值計量該等租賃負債。於二零一九年一月一日，該等
     average lessee’s incremental borrowing rate applied to the lease liabilities          租賃負債適用的加權平均的承租人的增量借款利率為
     on 1 January 2019 was 4.78%.                                                          4.78%。

     The Group did not enter into any leases arrangement which previously                  本集團無前期被分類為融資租賃的租賃
     classified as finance leases.

(a) Practical expedients applied                                                     (a)   適用的實務變通
     In applying IFRS 16 for the first time, the Group has used the following              首次採用《國際財務報告準則第16號 － 租賃》時，本集
     practical expedients permitted by the standard:                                       團使用了準則允許的下列實務變通：

2.3 Principles of consolidation and equity accounting                                   2.3   合併與權益會計原則
2.3.1 Subsidiaries                                                                      2.3.1 子公司
      Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured entities) over which                子公司為本集團所控制的實體（包括結構化實體）。當
      the Group has control. The Group controls an entity where the Group is                  本集團從或有權從與該實體的參與中獲得變動收益並有
      exposed to, or has rights to, variable returns from its involvement with the            能力對這些收益產生影響時，則其控制該子公司。自控
      entity and has the ability to affect those returns through its power to direct          制權轉移至本集團之日起，子公司被合併。其自喪失實
      the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries are fully consolidated from the              際控制權之日起停止合併。
      date on which control is transferred to the Group. They are deconsolidated
      from the date that control ceases.

      The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for business                    本集團的業務合併採用會計購買法核算（見附註2.4)。
      combinations by the Group (refer to Note 2.4).

      Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised gains on transactions               集團內公司之間的交易、結餘及交易的未變現利得予以
      between group companies are eliminated. Unrealised losses are also                      對銷。未變現損失亦予以對銷，除非交易提供所轉撥資
      eliminated unless the transaction provides evidence of an impairment                    產的減值證據。子公司報告的數額已按需要作出改變，
      of the transferred asset. Accounting policies of subsidiaries have been                 以確保與本集團採用的政策符合一致。
      changed where necessary to ensure consistency with the policies adopted
      by the Group.

      Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of subsidiaries are                 子公司業績和股權中的非控股權益分別列示於合併利潤
      shown separately in the consolidated income statement, consolidated                     表、合併綜合收益表、合併股權變動表和合併財務狀況
      statement of comprehensive income, statement of changes in equity and                   表。
      consolidated statement of financial position respectively.

2.3.2 Associates                                                                        2.3.2 聯營
      Associates are all entities over which the Group has significant influence              聯營公司是集團對其有重大影響但不控制或聯合控制的
      but not control or joint control. This is generally the case where the                  所有實體。通常情況下，該集團擁有20%至50%的投票
      Group holds between 20% and 50% of the voting rights. Investments in                    權。聯營公司的投資在最初按成本確認後，以權益會計
      associates are accounted for using the equity method of accounting (see                 方法核算（見附註2.3.3)。
      Note 2.3.3 below), after initially being recognised at cost.

2.3.3 Equity method                                                                 2.3.3 權益法
     Under the equity method of accounting, the investments are initially                 權益法下，投資帳面價值最初以成本確認，之後卻按本
     recognised at cost and adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s share            集團持有投資對象得份額確認投資利潤或虧損以及其他
     of the post-acquisition profits or losses of the investee in profit or loss,         綜合收益。從聯營公司收到或應收的股息確認為投資帳
     and the Group’s share of movements in other comprehensive income                     面價值的減少。
     of the investee in other comprehensive income. Dividends received or
     receivable from associates are recognised as a reduction in the carrying
     amount of the investment.

     Where the Group’s share of losses in an equity-accounted investment                  若本集團在股本投資中所承擔的損失與本集團在該實體
     equals or exceeds its interest in the entity, including any other unsecured          （包括任何其他無擔保的長期應收賬款）的權益相等或
     long-term receivables, the Group does not recognise further losses,                  超過其權益，則本集團不承認進一步的損失，除非本集
     unless it has incurred obligations or made payments on behalf of the                 團已為該實體承擔了義務或代表該實體支付了款項。
     other entity.

     Unrealised gains on transactions between the Group and its associates                本集團與其聯營企業和合資企業之間的交易的未變現收
     are eliminated to the extent of the Group’s interest in these entities.              益，按本集團對這些實體的份額予以剔除。除非交易提
     Unrealised losses are also eliminated unless the transaction provides                供了轉讓資產減值的證據，否則未變現虧損也將被消
     evidence of an impairment of the asset transferred. Accounting policies of           除。股權投資公司的會計政策已根據需要進行了變更，
     equity-accounted investees have been changed where necessary to ensure               以確保與本集團所採取的會計政策保持一致。.
     consistency with the policies adopted by the Group.

     The carrying amount of equity-accounted investments is tested for                    股權投資的帳面價值按照附註2.13所述的政策進行減值
     impairment in accordance with the policy described in Note 2.13.                     測試。


2.3.4 Changes in ownership interests                                                2.3.4 所有權權益的變化
     The Group treats transactions with non-controlling interests that do                 本集團將不會導致控制權喪失的非控股股權交易視為與
     not result in a loss of control as transactions with equity owners of the            本集團股權所有者的交易。所有者權益的變化導致控制
     Group. A change in ownership interest results in an adjustment between               權益和非控制權益的帳面價值之間的調整，以反映它們
     the carrying amounts of the controlling and non-controlling interests to             在子公司中的相對權益。對非控股權益的調整金額與已
     reflect their relative interests in the subsidiary. Any difference between           付或已收的任何代價之間的任何差額，將在歸屬於本集
     the amount of the adjustment to non-controlling interests and any                    團股東的權益內的單獨儲備中確認。
     consideration paid or received is recognised in a separate reserve within
     equity attributable to owners of the Group.

2.4 Business combinations                                                               2.4   企業合併
      The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for all business                不論權益工具或其他資產是否被收購，會計的購買法都
      combinations, regardless of whether equity instruments or other assets are              被用於所有的企業合併。為收購附屬公司而轉讓的代價

      comprises the:

            consideration arrangement, and                                                        值，以及


      •     fair value of any pre-existing equity interest in the subsidiary.                 •   子公司現有權益的公允價值。


      Identifiable assets acquired and liabilities and contingent liabilities                 企業合併中取得的可辨認資產、負債和或有負債除個別
      assumed in a business combination are, with limited exceptions,                         情況外，最初按取得日的公允價值計量。本集團按按公
      measured initially at their fair values at the acquisition date. The Group              允價值或按非控股權益在被收購實體的可辨認淨資產中
      recognises any non-controlling interest in the acquired entity on an                    所佔的比例比例，按逐筆收購的方式確認其在被收購實
      acquisition-by-acquisition basis either at fair value or at the non-                    體中的任何非控股權益。
      controlling interest’s proportionate share of the acquired entity’s net
      identifiable assets.

2.7 Foreign currency translation                                                      2.7   外幣折算
(a) Functional and presentation currency                                              (a)   功能和列報貨幣
      Items included in the financial statements of each of the Group’s entities            本集團每個主體的財務報表所列項目均以該主體經營所
      are measured using the currency of the primary economic environment                   在的主要經濟環境的貨幣計量（「功能貨幣」）。合併財
      in which the entity operates (the “functional currency”). The consolidated            務報表以人民幣列報，人民幣為本公司的功能貨幣及本
      financial statements are presented in RMB, which is the Company’s                     集團的列報貨幣。
      functional and presentation currency.

(b) Transactions and balances                                                         (b)   交易及結餘
      Foreign currency transactions are translated into the functional currency             外幣交易採用交易或項目重新計量的估值日期的匯率換
      using the exchange rates at the dates of the transactions. Foreign                    算為功能貨幣。結算此等交易產生的匯兌利得和損失以
      exchange gains and losses resulting from the settlement of such                       及將外幣計值的貨幣資產和負債以年終匯率折算產生的
      transactions and from the translation of monetary assets and liabilities              匯兌利得和損失在合併損益表確認。
      denominated in foreign currencies at year end exchange rates are
      generally recognised in profit or loss.

      Foreign exchange gains and losses that relate to borrowings are presented             與借款有關的匯兌利得和損失在合併損益表內的「財務

      gains and losses are presented in the income statement on a net basis                 在合併損益表內的「其他收入及利得 － 淨額」中列報。
      within “Other income and gains – net”.

      Non-monetary items that are measured at fair value in a foreign currency              非貨幣性金融資產及負債（例如以公允價值計量且其變
      are translated using the exchange rates at the date when the fair value               動計入損益的權益）的折算差額列報為公允價值利得和
      was determined. Translation differences on assets and liabilities carried at          損失的一部份。非貨幣性金融資產及負債（例如分類為

      translation differences on non-monetary assets and liabilities such as

      or loss as part of the fair value gain or loss and translation differences
      on non-monetary assets such as equities classified as fair value through
      other comprehensive income are recognised in other comprehensive
      income.

2.13 Impairment of non-financial assets                                             2.13 非金融資產投資的減值
      Goodwill and intangible assets that have an indefinite useful life or               使用壽命不限定的無形資產或尚未可供使用的無形資產
      intangible assets not ready to use are not subject to amortisation and              無需攤銷，但每年須就減值進行測試。須作攤銷的資
      are tested annually for impairment. Assets are reviewed for impairment              產，當有事件出現或情況改變顯示帳面值可能無法收回
      whenever events or changes in circumstances indicate that the carrying              時就進行減值檢討。減值虧損按資產的帳面值超出其可
      amount may not be recoverable. An impairment loss is recognised for                 收回金額的差額確認。可收回金額以資產的公允價值扣
      the amount by which the asset’s carrying amount exceeds its recoverable             除銷售成本或使用價值兩者之間較高者為準。於評估減
      amount. The recoverable amount is the higher of an asset’s fair value less          值時，資產按可分開辨認現金流量（現金產出單元）的
      costs to sell and value in use. For the purposes of assessing impairment,           最低層次組合。除商譽外，已蒙受減值的非金融資產在
      assets are grouped at the lowest levels for which there are separately              每個報告日期均就減值是否可以轉回進行檢討。
      identifiable cash flows (cash-generating units). Non-financial assets
      other than goodwill that suffered an impairment are reviewed for possible
      reversal of the impairment at each reporting date.

2.14 Financial assets                                                                2.14 金融資產

      The Group classifies its financial assets in the following measurement              本集團按以下計量類別對金融資產進行分類：
      categories:

      •     those to be measured subsequently at fair value (either through OCI           •      後續以公允價值計量（且其變動計入其他綜合收
            or through profit or loss), and                                                      益或損益）的金融資產，及


      •     those to be measured at amortised cost.                                       •      以攤餘成本計量的金融資產。


      The classification depends on the entity’s business model for managing              該分類取決於主體管理金融資產的業務模式以及該資產
      the financial assets and the contractual terms of the cash flows.                   的合同現金流量特徵。


      For assets measured at fair value, gains and losses will either be recorded         對於以公允價值計量的金融資產，其利得和損失計入損
      in profit or loss or OCI. For investments in equity instruments that are            益或其他綜合收益。對於非交易性的權益工具投資，其
      not held for trading, this will depend on whether the Group has made                利得和損失的計量將取決於本集團在初始確認時是否作
      an irrevocable election at the time of initial recognition to account for           出不可撤銷的選擇而將其指定為以公允價值計量且其變
      the equity investment at fair value through other comprehensive income              動計入其他綜合收益。
      (FVOCI).

      The Group reclassifies debt investments when and only when its business             僅當管理該等資產的業務模式發生變化時，本集團才對
      model for managing those assets changes.                                            債權投資進行重分類。

2.15 Offsetting financial instruments                                                 2.15 抵銷金融工具
      Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net amount reported in           當有法定可執行權力可抵銷已確認金額，並有意圖按淨
      the statement of financial position when there is a legally enforceable              額基準結算或同時變現資產和結算負債時，金融資產與
      right to offset the recognised amounts and there is an intention to settle           負債可互相抵銷，並在財務狀況表報告其淨額。法定可
      on a net basis or realise the asset and settle the liability simultaneously.         執行權利必須不得依賴未來事件而定，而在一般業務過
      The legally enforceable right must not be contingent on future events and            程中以及倘公司或對手方一旦出現違約、無償債能力或
      must be enforceable in the normal course of business and in the event of             破產時，這也必須具有約束力。
      default, insolvency or bankruptcy of the Company or the counterparty.

2.21 Borrowings                                                                         2.21 借款
      Borrowings are recognised initially at fair value, net of transaction costs           一般及特定借款按公允價值並扣除產生的交易費用為初
      incurred. Borrowings are subsequently stated at amortised cost; any                   始確認金額。借款其後按攤銷成本列賬；所得款（扣除
      differences between proceeds (net of transaction cost) and the redemption             交易成本）與贖回價值的任何差額利用實際利率法於借
      value is recognised in the income statement over the period of the                    款期間內在損益表確認。
      borrowings using the effective interest method.

      Fees paid on the establishment of loan facilities are recognised as                   設立貸款融資時支付的費用倘部份或全部融資將會很可
      transaction costs of the loan to the extent that it is probable that some             能提取，該費用確認為貸款的交易費用。在此情況下，
      or all of the facility will be drawn down. In this case, the fee is deferred          費用遞延至貸款提取為止。如沒有證據證明部份或全部
      until the draw-down occurs. To the extent there is no evidence that it is             融資將會很可能被提取，則該費用資本化作為流動資金
      probable that some or all of the facility will be drawn down, the fee is              服務的預付款，並按有關的融資期間攤銷。
      capitalised as a pre-payment for liquidity services and amortised over the
      period of the facility to which it relates.

      The fair value of the liability portion of a convertible bond is determined           可換股債券負債部份的公允價值按照等同之非可換股債
      using a market interest rate for an equivalent non-convertible bond.                  券的市場利率釐定。此金額按攤銷成本記錄為負債，直
      This amount is recorded as a liability on an amortised cost basis until               至債券被兌換或到期而終止。餘下所得款分攤至兌換
      extinguished on conversion or maturity of the bond. The remainder of the              權。此款項在股東權益中確認和記錄，並扣除所得稅影
      proceeds is allocated to the conversion option. This is recognised and                響。
      included in shareholders’ equity, net of income tax effects.

      position when the obligation specified in the contract is discharged,                 狀況表中刪除。已消除或轉撥至另一方的金融負債的帳
      cancelled or expired. The difference between the carrying amount of a                 面值與已付對價的差額，包括任何已轉撥的非現金資產
      financial liability that has been extinguished or transferred to another party        或承擔的負債，在損益中確認為其他收入或融資成本。
      and the consideration paid, including any non-cash assets transferred

2.24 Current and deferred income tax                                                     2.24 當期及遞延所得稅
      The tax expense for the period comprises current and deferred tax. Tax is                本期間的稅項支出包括當期和遞延稅項。稅項在損益表
      recognised in the consolidated income statement, except to the extent that               中確認，但與在其他綜合收益中或直接在權益中確認的
      it relates to items recognised in other comprehensive income or directly                 項目有關者則除外。在該情況下，稅項亦分別在其他綜
      in equity. In this case, the tax is also recognised in other comprehensive               合收益或直接在權益中確認。
      income or directly in equity, respectively.

(a) Current income tax                                                                   (a)   當期所得稅
      The current income tax charge is calculated on the basis of the tax laws                 當期所得稅支出根據本公司的子公司及聯營經營及產生
      enacted or substantively enacted at the statement of financial position                  應課稅收入的國家於財務狀況表日已頒佈或實質上已頒
      date in the countries where the Company and its subsidiaries operate and                 佈的稅務法例計算。管理層就適用稅務法例解釋所規限
      generate taxable income. Management periodically evaluates positions                     的情況定期評估報稅表的狀況，並在適用情況下根據預
      taken in tax returns with respect to situations in which applicable tax                  期須向稅務機關支付的稅款設定準備。
      regulation is subject to interpretation. It establishes provisions where
      appropriate on the basis of amounts expected to be paid to the tax
      authorities.

(b) Deferred income tax                                                                  (b)   遞延所得稅
      Inside basis differences                                                                 內在差異
      Deferred income tax is recognised, using the liability method, on                        遞延所得稅利用負債法確認資產和負債的稅基與資產和
      temporary differences arising between the tax bases of assets and                        負債在合併財務報表的帳面值的差額而產生的暫時性差
      liabilities and their carrying amounts in the consolidated financial                     異。然而，在首次確認商譽時，不會確認遞延所得稅負
      statements. However, deferred tax liabilities are not recognised if they                 債。若遞延所得稅來自在交易（不包括業務合併）中對
      arise from the initial recognition of goodwill, the deferred income tax is               資產或負債的初始確認，而在交易時不影響會計損益或
      not accounted for if it arises from initial recognition of an asset or liability         應課稅利潤或損失，則不作記帳。遞延所得稅採用在財
      in a transaction other than a business combination that at the time of the               務狀況表日前已頒佈或實質上已頒佈，並在有關的遞延
      transaction affects neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss. Deferred              所得稅資產實現或遞延所得稅負債結算時預期將會適用
      income tax is determined using tax rates (and laws) that have been                       的稅率（及法例）而釐定。
      enacted or substantially enacted by the statement of financial position
      date and are expected to apply when the related deferred income tax asset
      is realised or the deferred income tax liability is settled.

      The deferred tax liability in relation to investment property that is                    以公允價值計量的與投資性物業相關的遞延所得稅負
      measured at fair value is determined assuming the property will be                       債，在假定該產權將完全通過出售而收回的前提下來確
      recovered entirely through sale.                                                         認。


      Deferred income tax assets are recognised only to the extent that it is                  遞延所得稅資產是就很可能有未來應課稅利潤而就此可
      probable that future taxable profit will be available against which the                  使用暫時性差異而確認。
      temporary differences can be utilised.

      of the temporary difference is controlled by the Group and it is probable              遞延所得稅負債。一般而言，本集團無法控制聯營的暫

      different taxable entities where there is an intention to settle the balances

2.27 Provisions                                                                       2.27 準備
      Provisions are recognised when: the Group has a present legal or                      當本集團因已發生的事件而產生現有的法律或推定義
      constructive obligation as a result of past events; it is probable that an            務；很可能需要有資源的流出以結算義務；及金額已被
      outflow of resources will be required to settle the obligation; and the               可靠估計時，當作出準備。準備不會就未來經營虧損確
      amount has been reliably estimated. Provisions are not recognised for                 認準備。
      future operating losses.

      Where there are a number of similar obligations, the likelihood that an               如有多項類似義務，其需要在結算中有資源流出的可能
      outflow will be required in settlement is determined by considering the               性，則可根據義務的類別整體考慮。即使在同一義務類
      class of obligations as a whole. A provision is recognised even if the                別所包含的任何一個項目相關的資源流出的可能性極
      likelihood of an outflow with respect to any one item included in the same            低，仍須確認準備。
      class of obligations may be small.

      Provisions are measured at the present value of the expenditures expected             準備採用稅前利率按照預期需結算有關義務的支出現值
      to be required to settle the obligation using a pre-tax rate that reflects            計量，該利率反映當時市場對金錢時間值和有關義務固
      current market assessments of the time value of money and the risks                   有風險的評估。隨著時間過去而增加的準備確認為利息
      specific to the obligation. The increase in the provision due to passage of           費用。
      time is recognised as interest expense.

    rewards of ownership which were classified as finance leases.                      險和報酬的、被分類為融資租賃的租賃。

    ownership were not transferred to the Group as lessee were classified as           團（作為承租人）的租賃被分類為經營租賃（附註36）。

    any incentives received from the lessor) were charged to profit or loss on         租賃期內按直線法計入損益。
    a straight-line basis over the period of the lease.

    a corresponding liability at the date at which the leased asset is available       用的當日將租賃確認為使用權資產和相應的負債。
    for use by the Group.

    allocates the consideration in the contract to the lease and non-lease             集團基於各租賃組成部份與非租賃組成部份的單獨價格

    separate lease and non-lease components and instead accounts for these             租賃組成部份，而是將其作為一項單一的租賃組成部份
    as a single lease component.                                                       進行會計處理。

2.32 Government grants                                                                 2.32 政府補助
      Grants from the government are recognised at their fair value where there            當能夠合理地保證政府補貼將可收取，而本集團將會符

      will comply with all attached conditions.                                            認入帳。


      Government grants relating to costs are deferred and recognised in the               與成本有關之政府補助遞延入帳，並按擬補償之成本配
      consolidated income statement over the period necessary to match them                合其所需期間在合併損益表中確認。
      with the costs that they are intended to compensate.

      Government grants relating to property, plant and equipment are included             與購買不動產、工廠及設備有關之政府補貼列入非流動
      in non-current liabilities as deferred government grants and are credited            負債作為遞延政府補貼，並按有關資產之預計年期以直
      to the consolidated income statement on a straight-line basis over the               線法在損益賬確認。
      expected lives of the related assets.

2.33 Dividend distribution                                                             2.33 股息分派
      Dividends distribution to the Company’s shareholders is recognised                   向本公司股東分派的股息在股息獲本公司股東或董事會
      as a liability in the Group’s and the Company’s financial statements                 批准的期間內於本集團及公司的財務報表內列為負債。
      in the period in which the dividends are approved by the Company’s
      shareholders or board of directors, where appropriate.

3   Financial risk management (continued)                                           3     財務風險管理（續）
3.2 Capital risk management                                                         3.2   資金風險管理
    The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard the                     本集團的資金管理政策，是保障集團能持續運營，以為
    Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order to provide returns            股東提供回報和為其他權利持有人提供利益，同時維持
    for owners and benefits for other stakeholders and to maintain an optimal             最佳的資本結構以降低資金成本。
    capital structure to reduce the cost of capital.

    In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may adjust            為了維持或調整資本結構，本集團可能會調整支付予股
    the amount of dividends paid to owners, return capital to shareholders,               東的股利數額、向股東退還資本、發行新股或出售資產
    issue new shares or sell assets to reduce debt.                                       以減低債務。


    Consistent with others in the industry, the Group monitors capital on the             與業內其他公司一樣，本集團利用資本負債比率監察其
    basis of the gearing ratio. This ratio is calculated as net debt divided by           資本。此比率按照債務淨額除以總資本計算。債務淨額
    total capital. Net debt is calculated as total borrowings (including “current         為總借款（包括合併財務狀況表所列的「流動及非流動
    and non-current borrowings” as shown in the consolidated statement                    借款」）減去現金和現金等價物。總資本為「權益」
                                                                                                                （如
    of financial position) less cash and cash equivalents. Total capital is               合併財務狀況表所列）加債務淨額。
    calculated as “equity” as shown in the consolidated statement of financial
    position plus net debt.

    The gearing ratios at 31 December 2019 and 2018 were as follows:                      在二零一九年及二零一八年十二月三十一日，本集團的

    Impairment test for goodwill                                                          商譽減值
    Goodwill arises from the Group’s acquisition of Shanghai Guanyi                       商譽來自於本集團對上海管易雲計算軟件有限公司的收
    Cloudcomputing Software Co., Ltd. (“Guanyi”) and was determined at the                購，其在二零一六年二月二十九日收購日確認。該數值
    date of acquisition on 29 February 2016, being the difference between                 為管易雲可辨認淨資產公允價值與支付對價的差額。對
    the purchase considerations and the fair value of net identifiable assets of          管易合併產生的商譽已經通過現金產出單元進行了減值
    Guanyi. Goodwill has been primarily allocated to the CGU of Guanyi for                測試。
    impairment testing.

    The recoverable amount of a CGU is determined based on value-in-use                   現金產出單元的可回收金額根據使用價值計算。計算方
    calculations. These calculations use pre-tax cash flow forecasts based                式利用稅前現金流量預測，依據管理層批核的五年期財
    on the financial budgets approved by management covering a five-year                  政預算。管理層採用的五年預算期限是根據管易的未來
    period. Management determined a forecast period of five years based on                發展趨勢以及行業經驗所預計。超過該五年期的現金流
    expected development trend of Guanyi and industry experience. Cash                    量採用以下所述的估計的永續增長率作出推算。該增長
    flows beyond the five-year period are extrapolated using the estimated                率不超過現金產出單元經營的企業管理雲服務業務的長
    terminal growth rate stated below. The terminal growth rate does not                  期平均增長率。所採用之貼現率尚未扣除稅項，且反應
    exceed the long-term average growth rate for the enterprise management                有關行業的具體風險。
    cloud services business in which the CGU operates. The discount rate
    used is pre-tax and reflects specific risks relating to the relevant industry.

    The key assumptions used for value-in-use calculations for 2019 and                   二零一九使用價值計算使用的主要假設如下：

      index or rate. Where considered necessary to reduce credit risk, the Group           信用風險時，要求租戶在租賃合同期內提供保證金。
      required the tenants to provide deposits for the term of lease contract.

      Although the Group is exposed to changes in the residual value at the end            雖然本集團在當前租賃期結束時，會面臨剩餘價值的變
      of the current leases, the Group typically enters into new operating leases          化，但本集團通常會進入新的經營租賃期，因此，在這
      and therefore will not immediately realise any reduction in residual value           些租賃期結束時，本集團不會立即意識到剩餘價值的任

    The Group’s finance department includes a team that reviews the                                               本集團財務部設有一個小組，專責就財務報告目的對

    Discussions of valuation processes and results are held among the CFO,                                        CFO、估值小組與估值師最少每六個月開會一次，討論
    the valuation team and the valuers at least once every six months, in line                                    估值流程和相關結果。
    with the Group’s interim and annual reporting dates.

            prior year valuation report;

              Kingdee Tianyan Middleware, it is able to gain power over more than                           本集團憑著與其他投資者的協議，有能力取得超過半數
              one half of the voting rights by virtue of an agreement. Consequently, the                    投票權。因此，本集團將金蝶天燕仲介軟件，以及它的
              Group consolidates Kingdee Tianyan Middleware and its wholly-owned                            全資子公司北京金蝶天燕仲介軟件軟件系統有限公司，

      (b)     The Group does not have legal ownership in the equity of these operating                (b)   集團對上述運營實體並無法定所有權。集團自這些運營
              companies. The Group has entered into several contractual arrangements                        實體成立日起與它們及其股東訂立若干協定，該等協定
              with them and their equity holders since their incorporation. These                           不可撤銷且使得集團：
              contractual arrangements are irrevocable and enable the Group to:

              –     exercise effective financial and operational control over these                         －   對運營實體可行使有效的財務及經營控制權；
                    operating companies;

              –     exercise more than one half of the equity holders voting rights of                      －   對運營實體可行使超過半數以上的表決權；
                    these operating companies;

              –     receive substantially all of the economic interest returns generated by                 －   對運營實體進行業務支援從而取得其運營產生的
                    these operating companies in consideration for the business support;                        全部經濟利益；

              –     obtain an irrevocable and exclusive right to purchase the entire equity                 －   對運營實體的股權具有不可撤銷的獨家購買權；
                    interest in these operating companies from the equity holders;

              –     obtain a pledge over the entire equity interest of these operating                      －   從其股東獲得運營實體的股權作為協定下應付集
                    companies from their equity holders as collateral for all the payments                      團款項的抵押。
                    due to the Group and as security for performance of obligations under
                    the contractual arrangements.

              As a result of such contractual arrangements, the Group has rights to                         根據這些協定的約定，本集團享有可變回報，且有能力
              variable returns from its involvement with them and has the ability to affect                 透過其對這些運營實體（「結構化主體」）的權力影響這
              those returns through its power over these operating companies (“VIEs”)                       些回報從而實現控制。因此，本集團合併財務報表將它
              and is considered to control them. Consequently, the Group regards them as                    們視為控制結構化主體。
              controlled structured entities in the consolidated financial statements.

              Nevertheless, the contractual arrangements may not be as effective as direct                  該種協議控股或不如本集團通過法定所有權直接控股這
              legal ownership in providing the Group with direct control over these VIEs                    些結構化主體有效，中國法律體系的不確定性可能影響
              and uncertainties presented by the PRC legal system could impede the                          本集團對結構化主體的經營成果、資產、負債的收益
              Group’s beneficiary rights of the results, assets and liabilities of these VIEs.              權。本集團認為該協議控股符合中國相關法律法規並具
              The Group believes that these contractual arrangements are in compliance                      有法律效力。
              with relevant PRC laws and regulations and are legally binding and
              enforceable.
